{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/repositories/admins.ts",
  "content": "import { and, asc, eq, isNull, or, sql } from \"drizzle-orm\"\n\nimport type { OfficialTier } from \"~~/shared/approvals/state\"\nimport { approvalAdmins, memberships, users } from \"~~/shared/db/schema\"\nimport type { CategoryLevel } from \"~~/shared/taxonomy\"\nimport { categoryAncestors } from \"~~/shared/taxonomy\"\nimport type { ExtensionCategory } from \"~~/shared/types\"\n\nimport type { Transactable } from \"./types\"\n\n// `approval_admins` table accessor. Replaces the pre-redesign\n// `approval_reviewers` repo (`reviewers.ts`). A row grants both admin\n// authority over its covered shadow (descendant cells on either the\n// tier-column or category-level axis) and the duty to decide approval\n// requests routed into that shadow. See ADR-0001 for the cover rules.\n//\n// Cell key (5-dim): (extensionCategory, tier, productLineId?, level, key).\n\nexport type { OfficialTier }\n\nexport interface AdminCell {\n  extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n  tier: OfficialTier\n  productLineId: string | null\n  categoryLevel: CategoryLevel\n  categoryKey: string\n}\n\nexport interface AdminRow extends AdminCell {\n  id: string\n  userId: string\n  userEmail: string\n  userName: string | null\n  createdAt: Date\n}\n\nexport interface InsertAdminRow extends AdminCell {\n  id: string\n  userId: string\n}\n\n// Full matrix view, joined with `users` so the matrix UI can render\n// admin chips with a recognisable label without an extra round-trip.\n// Sort order matches the UI's row→column→key ordering: extension\n// category first (the active tab), then column-tier shape (Company\n// before any product line), then the vertical hierarchy.\nexport async function listMatrix(db: Transactable): Promise<AdminRow[]> {\n  return db\n    .select({\n      id: approvalAdmins.id,\n      extensionCategory: approvalAdmins.extensionCategory,\n      tier: approvalAdmins.tier,\n      productLineId: approvalAdmins.productLineId,\n      categoryLevel: approvalAdmins.categoryLevel,\n      categoryKey: approvalAdmins.categoryKey,\n      userId: approvalAdmins.userId,\n      userEmail: users.email,\n      userName: users.name,\n      createdAt: approvalAdmins.createdAt,\n    })\n    .from(approvalAdmins)\n    .innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, approvalAdmins.userId))\n    .orderBy(\n      asc(approvalAdmins.extensionCategory),\n      asc(approvalAdmins.tier),\n      asc(approvalAdmins.productLineId),\n      asc(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel),\n      asc(approvalAdmins.categoryKey),\n      asc(users.email),\n    )\n}\n\n// All admin cells the given user holds. Drives both the\n// `requireCellAdmin` covering-cell annotation in the matrix list\n// response and the reviewer queue's \"what requests cover my cells\"\n// expansion in `server/repositories/approvals.ts`.\nexport async function listCellsForUser(\n  db: Transactable,\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<AdminCell[]> {\n  return db\n    .select({\n      extensionCategory: approvalAdmins.extensionCategory,\n      tier: approvalAdmins.tier,\n      productLineId: approvalAdmins.productLineId,\n      categoryLevel: approvalAdmins.categoryLevel,\n      categoryKey: approvalAdmins.categoryKey,\n    })\n    .from(approvalAdmins)\n    .where(eq(approvalAdmins.userId, userId))\n}\n\n// Column-tier ancestors of a target cell — `(company, null)` is the\n// universal cover when the target is a product-line cell; the target\n// itself is always its own ancestor.\nfunction columnAncestors(\n  tier: OfficialTier,\n  productLineId: string | null,\n): Array<{ tier: OfficialTier; productLineId: string | null }> {\n  if (tier === \"productLine\") {\n    return [\n      { tier: \"productLine\", productLineId },\n      { tier: \"company\", productLineId: null },\n    ]\n  }\n  return [{ tier: \"company\", productLineId: null }]\n}\n\n// Has the given user any admin row that covers the target cell? The\n// covering set is at most 6 cells (≤2 column shapes × ≤3 category\n// shapes) — built in code and OR-ed into a single index probe on\n// `idx_admins_user`. Returns `true` for the first matching row.\n//\n// Used by both `requireCellAdmin` (the matrix-edit gate) and the\n// reviewer queue's per-request covering check. Cross-extensionCategory\n// authority does not exist — each ext-type matrix is independent —\n// so the candidate cells all share the target's extensionCategory.\nexport async function findCoveringAdmin(\n  db: Transactable,\n  userId: string,\n  target: {\n    extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n    tier: OfficialTier\n    productLineId: string | null\n    categoryLevel: CategoryLevel\n    categoryKey: string\n  },\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const colShapes = columnAncestors(target.tier, target.productLineId)\n  const catShapes = categoryAncestors(target.categoryLevel, target.categoryKey)\n\n  const colExpr = or(\n    ...colShapes.map((c) =>\n      c.productLineId === null\n        ? and(eq(approvalAdmins.tier, c.tier), isNull(approvalAdmins.productLineId))\n        : and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.tier, c.tier),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.productLineId, c.productLineId),\n          ),\n    ),\n  )\n  const catExpr = or(\n    ...catShapes.map((k) =>\n      and(\n        eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, k.level),\n        eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, k.key),\n      ),\n    ),\n  )\n\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({ id: approvalAdmins.id })\n    .from(approvalAdmins)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(approvalAdmins.userId, userId),\n        eq(approvalAdmins.extensionCategory, target.extensionCategory),\n        colExpr,\n        catExpr,\n      ),\n    )\n    .limit(1)\n  return !!row\n}\n\n// Reviewer queue's per-request covering check (used at decide time):\n// is this user an admin whose shadow covers the given request? The\n// request's `l2` is optional — when null we only consider the\n// (all, '*') and (macro, subCat) candidates; a `micro` admin cell is\n// unreachable for an l2-less request, by construction.\nexport async function isAdminCoveringRequest(\n  db: Transactable,\n  userId: string,\n  request: {\n    extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n    tier: OfficialTier\n    productLineId: string | null\n    subCat: string\n    l2: string | null\n  },\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  // Treat the request as a `micro` cell when it carries an l2 (and the\n  // ancestor walk reflows up to macro+all); otherwise treat as `macro`.\n  return findCoveringAdmin(db, userId, {\n    extensionCategory: request.extensionCategory,\n    tier: request.tier,\n    productLineId: request.productLineId,\n    categoryLevel: request.l2 ? \"micro\" : \"macro\",\n    categoryKey: request.l2 ?? request.subCat,\n  })\n}\n\n// Fan-out across users who'll review the given pending request — used\n// by the notification consumer's \"tell the reviewers\" path. Returns\n// distinct userIds; the per-tier covering rules are the same as\n// `isAdminCoveringRequest` but evaluated as a SET rather than a probe.\nexport async function findAdminsForRequest(\n  db: Transactable,\n  request: {\n    extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n    tier: OfficialTier\n    productLineId: string | null\n    subCat: string\n    l2: string | null\n  },\n): Promise<string[]> {\n  const colShapes = columnAncestors(request.tier, request.productLineId)\n  const catShapes = categoryAncestors(\n    request.l2 ? \"micro\" : \"macro\",\n    request.l2 ?? request.subCat,\n  )\n\n  const colExpr = or(\n    ...colShapes.map((c) =>\n      c.productLineId === null\n        ? and(eq(approvalAdmins.tier, c.tier), isNull(approvalAdmins.productLineId))\n        : and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.tier, c.tier),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.productLineId, c.productLineId),\n          ),\n    ),\n  )\n  const catExpr = or(\n    ...catShapes.map((k) =>\n      and(\n        eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, k.level),\n        eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, k.key),\n      ),\n    ),\n  )\n\n  const rows = await db\n    .selectDistinct({ userId: approvalAdmins.userId })\n    .from(approvalAdmins)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(approvalAdmins.extensionCategory, request.extensionCategory),\n        colExpr,\n        catExpr,\n      ),\n    )\n  return rows.map((r) => r.userId)\n}\n\nexport async function insertAdmin(\n  db: Transactable,\n  row: InsertAdminRow,\n): Promise<void> {\n  // No `target` on the conflict clause — the table carries two partial\n  // unique indexes (one per tier) and Drizzle can't synthesise a typed\n  // target across both. The CHECK + partial uniques still de-dupe at the\n  // DB layer; this just makes a duplicate insert a no-op instead of an\n  // error (same pattern as the pre-redesign repo).\n  await db.insert(approvalAdmins).values(row).onConflictDoNothing()\n}\n\nexport async function deleteAdmin(\n  db: Transactable,\n  id: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  await db.delete(approvalAdmins).where(eq(approvalAdmins.id, id))\n}\n\n// Single-row fetch used by the unassign endpoint so it can authorise\n// against the row's own cell coords before deleting.\nexport async function findAdminById(\n  db: Transactable,\n  id: string,\n): Promise<AdminRow | null> {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      id: approvalAdmins.id,\n      extensionCategory: approvalAdmins.extensionCategory,\n      tier: approvalAdmins.tier,\n      productLineId: approvalAdmins.productLineId,\n      categoryLevel: approvalAdmins.categoryLevel,\n      categoryKey: approvalAdmins.categoryKey,\n      userId: approvalAdmins.userId,\n      userEmail: users.email,\n      userName: users.name,\n      createdAt: approvalAdmins.createdAt,\n    })\n    .from(approvalAdmins)\n    .innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, approvalAdmins.userId))\n    .where(eq(approvalAdmins.id, id))\n    .limit(1)\n  return row ?? null\n}\n\n// Super-admin check. A user is a super-admin if any of their\n// memberships carries the `superAdmin` role. Decoupled from a specific\n// org so seeded super-admins keep their authority even when the\n// default org is renamed.\nexport async function isSuperAdmin(\n  db: Transactable,\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({ id: memberships.id })\n    .from(memberships)\n    .where(\n      and(eq(memberships.userId, userId), eq(memberships.role, \"superAdmin\")),\n    )\n    .limit(1)\n  return !!row\n}\n\n// Defensive default — used only by tests that want to keep the\n// existing `sql` helper imported when none of the queries above are\n// being mocked. Keeps tree-shaking honest in production builds.\nexport const __sql = sql\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/utils/auth.ts",
  "content": "import { betterAuth } from \"better-auth\"\nimport { drizzleAdapter } from \"better-auth/adapters/drizzle\"\nimport { createError, getRequestHeaders, type H3Event } from \"h3\"\n\nimport type { OfficialTier } from \"~~/shared/approvals/state\"\n\nimport {\n  findCoveringAdmin,\n  isSuperAdmin,\n} from \"~~/server/repositories/admins\"\nimport {\n  accounts,\n  sessions,\n  users,\n  verifications,\n} from \"~~/shared/db/schema/auth\"\nimport type { CategoryLevel } from \"~~/shared/taxonomy\"\nimport type { ExtensionCategory } from \"~~/shared/types\"\n\nimport { useDb } from \"./db\"\n\nfunction makeAuth() {\n  const db = useDb()\n  return betterAuth({\n    database: drizzleAdapter(db, {\n      provider: \"pg\",\n      schema: {\n        user: users,\n        session: sessions,\n        account: accounts,\n        verification: verifications,\n      },\n    }),\n    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },\n    user: {\n      additionalFields: {\n        locale: {\n          type: \"string\",\n          required: false,\n          defaultValue: \"en\",\n          input: false,\n        },\n        themePreference: {\n          type: \"string\",\n          required: false,\n          defaultValue: \"ivory\",\n          input: false,\n        },\n        defaultDeptId: {\n          type: \"string\",\n          required: false,\n          input: false,\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  })\n}\n\ntype AuthInstance = ReturnType<typeof makeAuth>\n\nlet _auth: AuthInstance | undefined\n\nexport function useAuthServer(): AuthInstance {\n  if (!_auth) _auth = makeAuth()\n  return _auth\n}\n\ntype SessionPayload = Awaited<\n  ReturnType<AuthInstance[\"api\"][\"getSession\"]>\n>\nexport type SessionUser = NonNullable<SessionPayload>[\"user\"]\n\nfunction toFetchHeaders(raw: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>): Headers {\n  const headers = new Headers()\n  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(raw)) {\n    if (value === undefined) continue\n    headers.append(key, Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(\", \") : value)\n  }\n  return headers\n}\n\nexport async function getSessionUser(event: H3Event): Promise<SessionUser | null> {\n  const auth = useAuthServer()\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({\n    headers: toFetchHeaders(getRequestHeaders(event)),\n  })\n  return session?.user ?? null\n}\n\nexport async function requireUser(event: H3Event): Promise<SessionUser> {\n  const user = await getSessionUser(event)\n  if (!user) {\n    throw createError({ statusCode: 401, statusMessage: \"Unauthenticated\" })\n  }\n  return user\n}\n\n// Super-admin gate for the reviewer-matrix admin surface. Returns the\n// session user when they hold any membership with role='superAdmin';\n// throws 401 when unauthenticated and 403 otherwise. The membership\n// lookup goes through the admins repo so the policy lives in one\n// place — see `server/repositories/admins.ts`.\nexport async function requireSuperAdmin(event: H3Event): Promise<SessionUser> {\n  const user = await requireUser(event)\n  if (!(await isSuperAdmin(useDb(), user.id))) {\n    throw createError({ statusCode: 403, statusMessage: \"Forbidden\" })\n  }\n  return user\n}\n\n// Cell-aware authorisation for matrix edits, redesigned around the\n// 5-coord cell key. Encodes ADR-0001's 2026-06-09b redesign:\n//   - super-admins can edit every cell;\n//   - any user who holds an admin row whose covered shadow includes\n//     the target cell can edit it. Coverage is the 2-dim cover\n//     relation: (T,C) ⊇ (T',C') AND (L,K) ⊇ (L',K'), where ⊇ on the\n//     column-tier axis is \"company covers all PLs (and itself)\" and\n//     ⊇ on the category axis is the all → macro → micro ancestor walk.\n// Callers pass the target cell explicitly — for `unassign`, load the\n// target row first so the gate authorises against that row's coordinates\n// rather than the caller's request body.\nexport async function requireCellAdmin(\n  event: H3Event,\n  cell: {\n    extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n    tier: OfficialTier\n    productLineId: string | null\n    categoryLevel: CategoryLevel\n    categoryKey: string\n  },\n): Promise<SessionUser> {\n  const user = await requireUser(event)\n  const db = useDb()\n  if (await isSuperAdmin(db, user.id)) return user\n  if (await findCoveringAdmin(db, user.id, cell)) return user\n  throw createError({ statusCode: 403, statusMessage: \"not_authorized\" })\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/repositories/approvals.ts",
  "content": "import { and, desc, eq, inArray, isNull, or, sql } from \"drizzle-orm\"\n\nimport type {\n  ApprovalStatus,\n  OfficialTier,\n} from \"~~/shared/approvals/state\"\nimport {\n  approvalAdmins,\n  approvalRequests,\n  extensions,\n} from \"~~/shared/db/schema\"\nimport type { ExtensionCategory } from \"~~/shared/types\"\n\nimport type { Transactable } from \"./types\"\n\n// `approval_requests` table accessor. Used by the approval orchestrator to\n// drive the publisher submit / reviewer decide / publisher withdraw flow.\n// `ApprovalStatus` and `OfficialTier` are re-exported so callers that\n// already depend on the repository module don't need a second import.\n\nexport type { ApprovalStatus, OfficialTier }\n\nexport interface ApprovalRequestRow {\n  id: string\n  extensionId: string\n  // Snapshot of the extension's category at submission time. Frozen\n  // here so the routing fan-out doesn't have to join `extensions`.\n  extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n  requestedTier: OfficialTier\n  subCat: string\n  // Snapshot of the extension's l2 leaf, when classified. Null for\n  // extensions that only carry a macro classification — routing fan-out\n  // simply omits the `micro` candidate when this is null.\n  l2: string | null\n  productLineId: string | null\n  requestedByUserId: string\n  reason: string | null\n  status: ApprovalStatus\n  decidedByUserId: string | null\n  decidedAt: Date | null\n  reviewerNote: string | null\n  createdAt: Date\n  updatedAt: Date\n}\n\nconst fullSelect = {\n  id: approvalRequests.id,\n  extensionId: approvalRequests.extensionId,\n  extensionCategory: approvalRequests.extensionCategory,\n  requestedTier: approvalRequests.requestedTier,\n  subCat: approvalRequests.subCat,\n  l2: approvalRequests.l2,\n  productLineId: approvalRequests.productLineId,\n  requestedByUserId: approvalRequests.requestedByUserId,\n  reason: approvalRequests.reason,\n  status: approvalRequests.status,\n  decidedByUserId: approvalRequests.decidedByUserId,\n  decidedAt: approvalRequests.decidedAt,\n  reviewerNote: approvalRequests.reviewerNote,\n  createdAt: approvalRequests.createdAt,\n  updatedAt: approvalRequests.updatedAt,\n}\n\nexport interface InsertApprovalRequest {\n  id: string\n  extensionId: string\n  extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n  requestedTier: OfficialTier\n  subCat: string\n  l2: string | null\n  productLineId: string | null\n  requestedByUserId: string\n  reason: string | null\n}\n\nexport async function insertRequest(\n  db: Transactable,\n  row: InsertApprovalRequest,\n): Promise<ApprovalRequestRow> {\n  const [created] = await db\n    .insert(approvalRequests)\n    .values(row)\n    .returning(fullSelect)\n  if (!created) throw new Error(\"approval request insert vanished\")\n  return created\n}\n\nexport async function findById(\n  db: Transactable,\n  id: string,\n): Promise<ApprovalRequestRow | null> {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select(fullSelect)\n    .from(approvalRequests)\n    .where(eq(approvalRequests.id, id))\n    .limit(1)\n  return row ?? null\n}\n\n// Bulk variant of `findPendingByExtension` for the publisher dashboard —\n// the listing already loads many extensions, so we fan in a single query\n// rather than N round-trips and return a Map<extId, pendingRequest>.\nexport async function findPendingForExtensions(\n  db: Transactable,\n  extensionIds: string[],\n): Promise<Map<string, ApprovalRequestRow>> {\n  if (extensionIds.length === 0) return new Map()\n  const rows = await db\n    .select(fullSelect)\n    .from(approvalRequests)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        inArray(approvalRequests.extensionId, extensionIds),\n        eq(approvalRequests.status, \"pending\"),\n      ),\n    )\n  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.extensionId, r]))\n}\n\n// Used by the at-most-one-pending guard in the submit orchestrator.\nexport async function findPendingByExtension(\n  db: Transactable,\n  extensionId: string,\n): Promise<ApprovalRequestRow | null> {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select(fullSelect)\n    .from(approvalRequests)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(approvalRequests.extensionId, extensionId),\n        eq(approvalRequests.status, \"pending\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .limit(1)\n  return row ?? null\n}\n\n// Publisher's \"my requests\" view.\nexport async function listForPublisher(\n  db: Transactable,\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ApprovalRequestRow[]> {\n  return db\n    .select(fullSelect)\n    .from(approvalRequests)\n    .where(eq(approvalRequests.requestedByUserId, userId))\n    .orderBy(desc(approvalRequests.createdAt))\n}\n\n// Reviewer queue — pending requests covered by the user's admin cells.\n// Implemented as a single JOIN between approval_admins and\n// approval_requests on the cover relation, so coverage logic lives in\n// one SQL clause rather than being recomputed per-cell in the\n// orchestrator.\n//\n// Cover relation (per the redesigned 5-coord cell):\n//   - extensionCategory and tier must match exactly;\n//   - productLineId matches via the (company, NULL) ⊇ (productLine, X)\n//     rule: a company-tier admin covers every PL request, and a PL admin\n//     covers only same-PL requests;\n//   - category dimension covers via the all → macro → micro walk:\n//     'all' covers everything; 'macro' covers requests with matching\n//     subCat (l2 may be anything, including NULL); 'micro' covers only\n//     requests where l2 matches exactly.\n//\n// DISTINCT on r.id de-dupes the case where the same user holds two\n// admin rows that both cover the same request (e.g. (all,'*') AND\n// (macro, subCat)). The pattern mirrors today's Set-based dedup in\n// `listReviewerQueue` and stays consistent across the orchestrator.\nexport async function listPendingForUser(\n  db: Transactable,\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ApprovalRequestRow[]> {\n  return db\n    .selectDistinct(fullSelect)\n    .from(approvalRequests)\n    .innerJoin(\n      approvalAdmins,\n      and(\n        eq(approvalAdmins.userId, userId),\n        eq(approvalAdmins.extensionCategory, approvalRequests.extensionCategory),\n        eq(approvalAdmins.tier, approvalRequests.requestedTier),\n        // Column-tier cover: same product line, or the company wildcard.\n        or(\n          eq(approvalAdmins.productLineId, approvalRequests.productLineId),\n          and(\n            isNull(approvalAdmins.productLineId),\n            isNull(approvalRequests.productLineId),\n          ),\n          and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.tier, \"company\"),\n            isNull(approvalAdmins.productLineId),\n          ),\n        ),\n        // Category cover: all/macro/micro per the level.\n        or(\n          and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, \"all\"),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, \"*\"),\n          ),\n          and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, \"macro\"),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, approvalRequests.subCat),\n          ),\n          and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, \"micro\"),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, approvalRequests.l2),\n          ),\n        ),\n      ),\n    )\n    .where(eq(approvalRequests.status, \"pending\"))\n    .orderBy(desc(approvalRequests.createdAt))\n}\n\n// Super-admin variant — every pending request, no JOIN. Kept separate\n// from `listPendingForUser` so the trust model is explicit at the call\n// site (the orchestrator picks one or the other based on the\n// super-admin check).\nexport async function listAllPending(\n  db: Transactable,\n): Promise<ApprovalRequestRow[]> {\n  return db\n    .select(fullSelect)\n    .from(approvalRequests)\n    .where(eq(approvalRequests.status, \"pending\"))\n    .orderBy(desc(approvalRequests.createdAt))\n}\n\nexport interface DecidePatch {\n  status: \"approved\" | \"rejected\"\n  decidedByUserId: string\n  decidedAt: Date\n  reviewerNote: string | null\n}\n\n// Optimistic-locking variant: only updates a request that's still pending.\n// Returns the number of affected rows so the orchestrator can detect a\n// race (a second reviewer that committed between the orchestrator's\n// pre-check and this UPDATE) and surface it as `request_not_pending`.\nexport async function applyDecision(\n  db: Transactable,\n  id: string,\n  patch: DecidePatch,\n): Promise<number> {\n  const updated = await db\n    .update(approvalRequests)\n    .set({ ...patch, updatedAt: new Date() })\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(approvalRequests.id, id),\n        eq(approvalRequests.status, \"pending\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .returning({ id: approvalRequests.id })\n  return updated.length\n}\n\n// Same optimistic-locking shape as `applyDecision` — only a pending\n// request can be withdrawn, and the affected row count tells the caller\n// whether the transition actually fired.\nexport async function applyWithdraw(\n  db: Transactable,\n  id: string,\n  decidedAt: Date,\n): Promise<number> {\n  const updated = await db\n    .update(approvalRequests)\n    .set({ status: \"withdrawn\", decidedAt, updatedAt: new Date() })\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(approvalRequests.id, id),\n        eq(approvalRequests.status, \"pending\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .returning({ id: approvalRequests.id })\n  return updated.length\n}\n\n// Companion mutator for the approve path — stamps `officialTier` and the\n// matching `productLineId` (null for company tier) on the parent extension.\n// Lives here (rather than in `extensions.ts`) because the approval\n// orchestrator is the only legitimate writer; keeping the call adjacent to\n// the request mutation makes that intent obvious. The DB CHECK enforces\n// productLineId is non-null iff tier='productLine', so a bad pair from a\n// caller is rejected at the boundary. Also clears the revocation audit\n// fields in the same UPDATE — the invariant is \"you're either currently\n// Official with no pending revocation explanation, or Unofficial\".\nexport async function setExtensionOfficialTier(\n  db: Transactable,\n  extensionId: string,\n  tier: OfficialTier,\n  productLineId: string | null,\n): Promise<void> {\n  await db\n    .update(extensions)\n    .set({\n      officialTier: tier,\n      productLineId,\n      revokedAt: null,\n      revokedByUserId: null,\n      revocationNote: null,\n      updatedAt: new Date(),\n    })\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n}\n\n// Companion mutator for the revoke path — atomically clears\n// `officialTier` and `productLineId` while writing the audit trio\n// (`revokedAt`, `revokedByUserId`, `revocationNote`). The orchestrator's\n// pre-check already confirmed the row was Official; this method's\n// returned affected-row count is the race-safe equivalent of a CAS,\n// surfacing \"someone else already revoked / re-approved\" as a 0.\nexport interface RevocationPatch {\n  revokedAt: Date\n  revokedByUserId: string\n  revocationNote: string\n}\nexport async function applyRevocation(\n  db: Transactable,\n  extensionId: string,\n  patch: RevocationPatch,\n): Promise<number> {\n  const updated = await db\n    .update(extensions)\n    .set({\n      officialTier: null,\n      productLineId: null,\n      revokedAt: patch.revokedAt,\n      revokedByUserId: patch.revokedByUserId,\n      revocationNote: patch.revocationNote,\n      updatedAt: new Date(),\n    })\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensions.id, extensionId),\n        sql`${extensions.officialTier} IS NOT NULL`,\n      ),\n    )\n    .returning({ id: extensions.id })\n  return updated.length\n}\n\n// Bulk lookup of `officialTier` for a set of extensions — used by the\n// publisher dashboard to render the per-row badge alongside any pending\n// requests in one round-trip.\nexport async function findTiersForExtensions(\n  db: Transactable,\n  extensionIds: string[],\n): Promise<Map<string, OfficialTier | null>> {\n  if (extensionIds.length === 0) return new Map()\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({ id: extensions.id, officialTier: extensions.officialTier })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .where(inArray(extensions.id, extensionIds))\n  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r.officialTier ?? null]))\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "// Reviewer queue — pending requests covered by the user's admin cells.\n// Implemented as a single JOIN between approval_admins and\n// approval_requests on the cover relation, so coverage logic lives in\n// one SQL clause rather than being recomputed per-cell in the\n// orchestrator.\n//\n// Cover relation (per the redesigned 5-coord cell):\n//   - extensionCategory, tier, and productLineId match exactly. The\n//     column-tier dimension is intentionally NOT widened for routing\n//     (company admins do not pick up product-line requests for review);\n//     the company→PL cover applies only to matrix-edit authority,\n//     handled by `findCoveringAdmin` in `admins.ts`.\n//   - category dimension covers via the all → macro → micro walk:\n//     'all' covers everything; 'macro' covers requests with matching\n//     subCat (l2 may be anything, including NULL); 'micro' covers only\n//     requests where l2 matches exactly (so an l2-less request never\n//     reaches a micro admin — the right semantic).\n//\n// DISTINCT on r.id de-dupes the case where the same user holds two\n// admin rows that both cover the same request (e.g. (all,'*') AND\n// (macro, subCat)). The pattern mirrors today's Set-based dedup in\n// `listReviewerQueue` and stays consistent across the orchestrator.\nexport async function listPendingForUser(\n  db: Transactable,\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ApprovalRequestRow[]> {\n  return db\n    .selectDistinct(fullSelect)\n    .from(approvalRequests)\n    .innerJoin(\n      approvalAdmins,\n      and(\n        eq(approvalAdmins.userId, userId),\n        eq(approvalAdmins.extensionCategory, approvalRequests.extensionCategory),\n        eq(approvalAdmins.tier, approvalRequests.requestedTier),\n        // Column-tier match — same PL on both sides, including the\n        // both-NULL case for company-tier rows. SQL's `=` returns NULL\n        // for NULL operands, so the IS NULL fallback is required.\n        or(\n          eq(approvalAdmins.productLineId, approvalRequests.productLineId),\n          and(\n            isNull(approvalAdmins.productLineId),\n            isNull(approvalRequests.productLineId),\n          ),\n        ),\n        // Category cover: all/macro/micro per the level.\n        or(\n          and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, \"all\"),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, \"*\"),\n          ),\n          and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, \"macro\"),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, approvalRequests.subCat),\n          ),\n          and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, \"micro\"),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, approvalRequests.l2),\n          ),\n        ),\n      ),\n    )\n    .where(eq(approvalRequests.status, \"pending\"))\n    .orderBy(desc(approvalRequests.createdAt))\n}"
}

  "old_string": "// Fan-out across users who'll review the given pending request — used\n// by the notification consumer's \"tell the reviewers\" path. Returns\n// distinct userIds; the per-tier covering rules are the same as\n// `isAdminCoveringRequest` but evaluated as a SET rather than a probe.\nexport async function findAdminsForRequest(\n  db: Transactable,\n  request: {\n    extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n    tier: OfficialTier\n    productLineId: string | null\n    subCat: string\n    l2: string | null\n  },\n): Promise<string[]> {\n  const colShapes = columnAncestors(request.tier, request.productLineId)\n  const catShapes = categoryAncestors(\n    request.l2 ? \"micro\" : \"macro\",\n    request.l2 ?? request.subCat,\n  )\n\n  const colExpr = or(\n    ...colShapes.map((c) =>\n      c.productLineId === null\n        ? and(eq(approvalAdmins.tier, c.tier), isNull(approvalAdmins.productLineId))\n        : and(\n            eq(approvalAdmins.tier, c.tier),\n            eq(approvalAdmins.productLineId, c.productLineId),\n          ),\n    ),\n  )\n  const catExpr = or(\n    ...catShapes.map((k) =>\n      and(\n        eq(approvalAdmins.categoryLevel, k.level),\n        eq(approvalAdmins.categoryKey, k.key),\n      ),\n    ),\n  )\n\n  const rows = await db\n    .selectDistinct({ userId: approvalAdmins.userId })\n    .from(approvalAdmins)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(approvalAdmins.extensionCategory, request.extensionCategory),\n        colExpr,\n        catExpr,\n      ),\n    )\n  return rows.map((r) => r.userId)\n}\n\nexport async function insertAdmin(",
  "new_string": "export async function insertAdmin("
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/utils/approvals.ts",
  "content": "import * as adminsRepo from \"~~/server/repositories/admins\"\nimport * as approvalsRepo from \"~~/server/repositories/approvals\"\nimport * as extensionsRepo from \"~~/server/repositories/extensions\"\nimport {\n  decideApprovalOutcome,\n  decideWithdrawOutcome,\n  type ApprovalAction,\n  type OfficialTier,\n} from \"~~/shared/approvals/state\"\nimport type { ExtensionCategory } from \"~~/shared/types\"\n\nimport { useDb } from \"./db\"\nimport { safeSend } from \"./inngest\"\n\n// Orchestrators over the approvals/admins/extensions repositories. Pure\n// transitions live in `shared/approvals/state.ts`; this file loads rows,\n// opens transactions, fires Inngest events, and maps domain errors to\n// HTTP-shaped codes the endpoints translate.\n\nexport type ApprovalErrorCode =\n  | \"extension_not_found\"\n  | \"not_publisher_owner\"\n  | \"extension_not_published\"\n  | \"request_not_found\"\n  | \"request_not_pending\"\n  | \"duplicate_pending_request\"\n  | \"not_reviewer\"\n  | \"not_requester\"\n  | \"missing_product_line\"\n  | \"unexpected_product_line\"\n  | \"extension_not_official\"\n\nexport class ApprovalError extends Error {\n  readonly code: ApprovalErrorCode\n  constructor(code: ApprovalErrorCode) {\n    super(code)\n    this.code = code\n    this.name = \"ApprovalError\"\n  }\n}\n\nexport interface SubmitParams {\n  extensionId: string\n  requestedTier: OfficialTier\n  subCat: string\n  productLineId: string | null\n  userId: string\n  reason: string | undefined\n}\n\nexport async function submitRequest(\n  params: SubmitParams,\n): Promise<approvalsRepo.ApprovalRequestRow> {\n  // Shape rule mirrors the DB CHECK: productLineId is present iff the\n  // requested tier is `productLine`. Surfacing both error codes lets the\n  // endpoint give a precise message instead of bouncing the raw 23514.\n  if (params.requestedTier === \"productLine\" && !params.productLineId) {\n    throw new ApprovalError(\"missing_product_line\")\n  }\n  if (params.requestedTier === \"company\" && params.productLineId) {\n    throw new ApprovalError(\"unexpected_product_line\")\n  }\n\n  const db = useDb()\n\n  const ext = await extensionsRepo.findById(db, params.extensionId)\n  if (!ext) throw new ApprovalError(\"extension_not_found\")\n  if (ext.publisherUserId !== params.userId) {\n    throw new ApprovalError(\"not_publisher_owner\")\n  }\n  if (ext.visibility !== \"published\") {\n    throw new ApprovalError(\"extension_not_published\")\n  }\n\n  const row = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    // Re-check inside the transaction — race window between the visibility\n    // load above and the insert below would otherwise let a duplicate slip\n    // through. PG isolation is `read committed` so the second check still\n    // sees a concurrent insert that already committed.\n    const existing = await approvalsRepo.findPendingByExtension(\n      tx,\n      params.extensionId,\n    )\n    if (existing) throw new ApprovalError(\"duplicate_pending_request\")\n\n    return approvalsRepo.insertRequest(tx, {\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      extensionId: params.extensionId,\n      // Snapshot the extension's category + l2 onto the request so\n      // routing fan-out doesn't have to join `extensions` and so the\n      // audit trail freezes them at submission time.\n      extensionCategory: ext.category as ExtensionCategory,\n      requestedTier: params.requestedTier,\n      subCat: params.subCat,\n      l2: ext.l2 ?? null,\n      productLineId: params.productLineId,\n      requestedByUserId: params.userId,\n      reason: params.reason ?? null,\n    })\n  })\n\n  // Best-effort: the request row is the source of truth and is already\n  // visible in the reviewer queue. A transient Inngest outage (or, in dev,\n  // a missing `inngest-cli dev` process) must not fail the user's submit\n  // after the row has been committed.\n  await safeSend({\n    name: \"extension/approval.requested\",\n    data: {\n      requestId: row.id,\n      extensionId: row.extensionId,\n      requestedTier: row.requestedTier,\n      subCat: row.subCat,\n      productLineId: row.productLineId,\n      requesterUserId: row.requestedByUserId,\n    },\n  })\n\n  return row\n}\n\nexport interface DecideParams {\n  requestId: string\n  action: ApprovalAction\n  reviewerUserId: string\n}\n\nexport async function decideRequest(\n  params: DecideParams,\n): Promise<approvalsRepo.ApprovalRequestRow> {\n  const db = useDb()\n\n  const current = await approvalsRepo.findById(db, params.requestId)\n  if (!current) throw new ApprovalError(\"request_not_found\")\n  if (current.status !== \"pending\") {\n    throw new ApprovalError(\"request_not_pending\")\n  }\n\n  // Super-admins can decide on any cell; otherwise the reviewer must\n  // hold an admin row whose covered shadow includes this request's\n  // cell. Coverage is computed in one query against the user's admin\n  // rows (see `isAdminCoveringRequest`).\n  const allowed =\n    (await adminsRepo.isSuperAdmin(db, params.reviewerUserId)) ||\n    (await adminsRepo.isAdminCoveringRequest(db, params.reviewerUserId, {\n      extensionCategory: current.extensionCategory,\n      tier: current.requestedTier,\n      productLineId: current.productLineId,\n      subCat: current.subCat,\n      l2: current.l2,\n    }))\n  if (!allowed) throw new ApprovalError(\"not_reviewer\")\n\n  const outcome = decideApprovalOutcome({\n    current: {\n      status: current.status,\n      requestedTier: current.requestedTier,\n    },\n    action: params.action,\n    reviewerUserId: params.reviewerUserId,\n    now: new Date(),\n  })\n\n  // Optimistic locking lives inside the repository's UPDATE — if a second\n  // reviewer decided between the pre-check above and this transaction, the\n  // affected row count comes back zero and we surface the race as\n  // `request_not_pending`.\n  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const updated = await approvalsRepo.applyDecision(\n      tx,\n      params.requestId,\n      outcome.request,\n    )\n    if (updated === 0) throw new ApprovalError(\"request_not_pending\")\n    if (outcome.extension) {\n      await approvalsRepo.setExtensionOfficialTier(\n        tx,\n        current.extensionId,\n        outcome.extension.officialTier,\n        current.productLineId,\n      )\n    }\n  })\n\n  const updated = await approvalsRepo.findById(db, params.requestId)\n  if (!updated) throw new Error(\"approval request vanished mid-decide\")\n\n  // Best-effort notification after commit; see submitRequest for rationale.\n  await safeSend({\n    name: \"extension/approval.decided\",\n    data: {\n      requestId: updated.id,\n      extensionId: updated.extensionId,\n      decision: outcome.request.status,\n      decidedByUserId: params.reviewerUserId,\n    },\n  })\n\n  return updated\n}\n\nexport interface WithdrawParams {\n  requestId: string\n  userId: string\n}\n\nexport async function withdrawRequest(\n  params: WithdrawParams,\n): Promise<approvalsRepo.ApprovalRequestRow> {\n  const db = useDb()\n\n  const current = await approvalsRepo.findById(db, params.requestId)\n  if (!current) throw new ApprovalError(\"request_not_found\")\n  if (current.requestedByUserId !== params.userId) {\n    throw new ApprovalError(\"not_requester\")\n  }\n  if (current.status !== \"pending\") {\n    throw new ApprovalError(\"request_not_pending\")\n  }\n\n  const outcome = decideWithdrawOutcome({\n    current: { status: current.status },\n    now: new Date(),\n  })\n  const affected = await approvalsRepo.applyWithdraw(\n    db,\n    params.requestId,\n    outcome.request.decidedAt,\n  )\n  if (affected === 0) throw new ApprovalError(\"request_not_pending\")\n\n  const updated = await approvalsRepo.findById(db, params.requestId)\n  if (!updated) throw new Error(\"approval request vanished mid-withdraw\")\n  return updated\n}\n\nexport interface RevokeTierParams {\n  extensionId: string\n  superAdminUserId: string\n  note: string\n}\n\nexport interface RevokeTierResult {\n  extensionId: string\n  revokedAt: Date\n}\n\n// Super-admin Revoke action from the detail page. Loads the extension,\n// pre-checks that it's actually Official, then atomically clears tier +\n// productLineId and writes the audit trio. The DB-level WHERE clause in\n// applyRevocation re-checks officialTier IS NOT NULL so a concurrent\n// revoke / re-approval loses the race and surfaces as\n// `extension_not_official` rather than silently clobbering the row.\n// Caller is expected to already be a super-admin (gated at the endpoint\n// via requireSuperAdmin) — we don't re-check here for the same reason the\n// reviewer-assign endpoint doesn't re-check its gate.\nexport async function revokeTier(\n  params: RevokeTierParams,\n): Promise<RevokeTierResult> {\n  const db = useDb()\n  const ext = await extensionsRepo.findById(db, params.extensionId)\n  if (!ext) throw new ApprovalError(\"extension_not_found\")\n  if (!ext.officialTier) throw new ApprovalError(\"extension_not_official\")\n\n  const revokedAt = new Date()\n  const affected = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    return approvalsRepo.applyRevocation(tx, params.extensionId, {\n      revokedAt,\n      revokedByUserId: params.superAdminUserId,\n      revocationNote: params.note,\n    })\n  })\n  if (affected === 0) throw new ApprovalError(\"extension_not_official\")\n\n  // Best-effort notification after commit; see submitRequest for rationale.\n  await safeSend({\n    name: \"extension/tier.revoked\",\n    data: {\n      extensionId: params.extensionId,\n      revokedByUserId: params.superAdminUserId,\n      revokedAt: revokedAt.toISOString(),\n      note: params.note,\n    },\n  })\n\n  return { extensionId: params.extensionId, revokedAt }\n}\n\n// Reviewer queue — pending requests in any cell this user covers.\n// Super-admins get the full pending list; everyone else gets the\n// JOIN-driven covered set (see `listPendingForUser`).\n//\n// Optional `filters` narrow the queue without changing the trust model:\n// they're applied as in-memory predicates so a reviewer never queries\n// outside their covered set. A filter that matches nothing yields [].\nexport interface ReviewerQueueFilters {\n  tier?: OfficialTier\n  subCat?: string\n  productLineId?: string\n  extensionCategory?: ExtensionCategory\n}\n\nexport async function listReviewerQueue(\n  userId: string,\n  filters: ReviewerQueueFilters = {},\n): Promise<approvalsRepo.ApprovalRequestRow[]> {\n  const db = useDb()\n  const rows = (await adminsRepo.isSuperAdmin(db, userId))\n    ? await approvalsRepo.listAllPending(db)\n    : await approvalsRepo.listPendingForUser(db, userId)\n\n  return rows.filter((r) => {\n    if (filters.tier && r.requestedTier !== filters.tier) return false\n    if (filters.subCat && r.subCat !== filters.subCat) return false\n    if (\n      filters.productLineId &&\n      r.productLineId !== filters.productLineId\n    ) {\n      return false\n    }\n    if (\n      filters.extensionCategory &&\n      r.extensionCategory !== filters.extensionCategory\n    ) {\n      return false\n    }\n    return true\n  })\n}\n\nexport async function listPublisherRequests(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<approvalsRepo.ApprovalRequestRow[]> {\n  return approvalsRepo.listForPublisher(useDb(), userId)\n}\n"
}

6	// Lightweight "what can I do in the admin surface" probe — backs the
7	// require-reviewer / require-super-admin client middleware so the pages
8	// can fail fast with 403 → redirect rather than rendering and then
9	// hitting an empty page.
10	//
11	// Returns safe defaults for anonymous callers rather than 401, because
12	// the detail page renders for anonymous browsers too and needs to read
13	// this endpoint without bailing out — the gates on the actual admin
14	// pages still throw 401 separately. `cells` is returned alongside the
15	// role booleans so the approvals queue page can render smart pickers
16	// bounded to what the viewer actually covers; an empty array means "no
17	// cells" rather than "any cell allowed".
18	export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
19	  // Per-user, cookie-dependent payload — same caching contract as

22	  setResponseHeader(event, "Vary", "Cookie")

26	  }

31	  ])

33	    isSuperAdmin: superAdmin,

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/admin/me.get.ts",
  "content": "import {\n  isSuperAdmin,\n  listCellsForUser,\n} from \"~~/server/repositories/admins\"\n\n// Lightweight \"what can I do in the admin surface\" probe — backs the\n// require-reviewer / require-super-admin client middleware so the pages\n// can fail fast with 403 → redirect rather than rendering and then\n// hitting an empty page.\n//\n// Returns safe defaults for anonymous callers rather than 401, because\n// the detail page renders for anonymous browsers too and needs to read\n// this endpoint without bailing out — the gates on the actual admin\n// pages still throw 401 separately. `cells` is returned alongside the\n// role booleans so the approvals queue page can render smart pickers\n// bounded to what the viewer actually covers; an empty array means \"no\n// cells\" rather than \"any cell allowed\".\nexport default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  // Per-user, cookie-dependent payload — same caching contract as\n  // /api/internal/auth/me.\n  setResponseHeader(event, \"Cache-Control\", \"private, no-store\")\n  setResponseHeader(event, \"Vary\", \"Cookie\")\n  const user = await getSessionUser(event)\n  if (!user) {\n    return { isSuperAdmin: false, isReviewer: false, cells: [] }\n  }\n  const db = useDb()\n  const [superAdmin, cells] = await Promise.all([\n    isSuperAdmin(db, user.id),\n    listCellsForUser(db, user.id),\n  ])\n  return {\n    isSuperAdmin: superAdmin,\n    isReviewer: superAdmin || cells.length > 0,\n    cells,\n  }\n})\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/admin/reviewers/index.get.ts",
  "content": "import {\n  findCoveringAdmin,\n  isSuperAdmin,\n  listCellsForUser,\n  listMatrix,\n} from \"~~/server/repositories/admins\"\nimport { listAllProductLines } from \"~~/server/repositories/productLines\"\n\n// Returns the matrix plus per-cell `canEdit` annotations and the\n// productLine list, so the admin UI can render the unified table and\n// grey non-editable cells in one round-trip. The access gate widens\n// from \"super-admin only\" to \"super-admin OR any matrix admin\" — a\n// non-super viewer sees the full read-only matrix and can only mutate\n// cells whose covering shadow they're in (see `requireCellAdmin`).\nexport default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  const user = await requireUser(event)\n  const db = useDb()\n  const [superAdmin, coveringCells] = await Promise.all([\n    isSuperAdmin(db, user.id),\n    listCellsForUser(db, user.id),\n  ])\n  if (!superAdmin && coveringCells.length === 0) {\n    throw createError({ statusCode: 403, statusMessage: \"Forbidden\" })\n  }\n\n  const [admins, productLines] = await Promise.all([\n    listMatrix(db),\n    listAllProductLines(db),\n  ])\n\n  // Per-row canEdit: super-admins everywhere, otherwise probe the\n  // covering relation against the row's own cell coords. The probe is\n  // one indexed query per row — cheap relative to the JOIN already\n  // running for listMatrix.\n  const annotated = await Promise.all(\n    admins.map(async (r) => ({\n      ...r,\n      canEdit:\n        superAdmin ||\n        (await findCoveringAdmin(db, user.id, {\n          extensionCategory: r.extensionCategory,\n          tier: r.tier,\n          productLineId: r.productLineId,\n          categoryLevel: r.categoryLevel,\n          categoryKey: r.categoryKey,\n        })),\n    })),\n  )\n\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    productLines,\n    admins: annotated,\n    viewer: {\n      isSuperAdmin: superAdmin,\n      coveringCells,\n    },\n  }\n})\n"
}

  "content": "import { insertAdmin } from \"~~/server/repositories/admins\"\nimport { AssignAdminSchema } from \"~~/shared/validators/approvals\"\n\n// Per-cell authorisation via the redesigned `requireCellAdmin` gate:\n// super-admins everywhere, plus any user whose admin shadow covers the\n// target cell. Coverage is computed in one indexed query — see\n// `findCoveringAdmin` in `server/repositories/admins.ts`. The\n// validator's refines already guarantee the (level, key) keyspace and\n// the productLineId iff-rule, so any failure past this point would be\n// a true 23514 not a Zod error.\nexport default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  const body = await readValidatedBody(event, (raw) =>\n    AssignAdminSchema.parse(raw),\n  )\n  await requireCellAdmin(event, {\n    extensionCategory: body.extensionCategory,\n    tier: body.tier,\n    productLineId: body.productLineId ?? null,\n    categoryLevel: body.categoryLevel,\n    categoryKey: body.categoryKey,\n  })\n\n  await insertAdmin(useDb(), {\n    id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n    extensionCategory: body.extensionCategory,\n    tier: body.tier,\n    productLineId: body.productLineId ?? null,\n    categoryLevel: body.categoryLevel,\n    categoryKey: body.categoryKey,\n    userId: body.userId,\n  })\n  return { ok: true }\n})\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/admin/reviewers/unassign.delete.ts",
  "content": "import {\n  deleteAdmin,\n  findAdminById,\n} from \"~~/server/repositories/admins\"\nimport { UnassignAdminSchema } from \"~~/shared/validators/approvals\"\n\n// Load the row first so the cell-aware gate authorises against the\n// row's own 5-coord cell, not against anything the caller supplies.\n// Without this, a non-super admin could uncover the id of any row by\n// guessing — the request body alone can't tell us which cell the id\n// belongs to.\nexport default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  const body = await readValidatedBody(event, (raw) =>\n    UnassignAdminSchema.parse(raw),\n  )\n  const row = await findAdminById(useDb(), body.id)\n  if (!row) {\n    throw createError({ statusCode: 404, statusMessage: \"admin_not_found\" })\n  }\n  await requireCellAdmin(event, {\n    extensionCategory: row.extensionCategory,\n    tier: row.tier,\n    productLineId: row.productLineId,\n    categoryLevel: row.categoryLevel,\n    categoryKey: row.categoryKey,\n  })\n  await deleteAdmin(useDb(), body.id)\n  return { ok: true }\n})\n"
}

9	// Mirror the listing rail's URL contract — the same query keys (tier,

11	// picker click triggers a refresh via the `query` reactive ref.
12	const listQuery = computed(() => ({

17	}))

24	  },

28	  {
29	    default: () => ({ ok: true, productLines: [] }),
30	  },
31	)
32	// admin/me returns the viewer's covered cells alongside the role flags so
33	// the smart pickers can offer only the subCats the viewer actually owns.
34	// Super-admins get every cell in the matrix (via `isSuperAdmin`); regular
35	// reviewers see their own assignment list.

43	      productLineId: string | null
44	    }>,

53	// signals "full grid" to the picker.

Omitting `allowedSubCats`\n// signals \"full grid\" to the picker.\nconst allowedSubCats = computed<readonly string[] | undefined>(() => {\n  if (adminMe.value.isSuperAdmin) return undefined\n  return Array.from(new Set(adminMe.value.cells.map((c) => c.subCat)))\n})",
  "new_string": "const { data: adminMe } = await useFetch(\"/api/internal/admin/me\", {\n  default: () => ({\n    isSuperAdmin: false,\n    isReviewer: false,\n    cells: [] as Array<{\n      extensionCategory: \"skills\" | \"mcp\" | \"slash\" | \"plugins\"\n      tier: \"productLine\" | \"company\"\n      productLineId: string | null\n      categoryLevel: \"all\" | \"macro\" | \"micro\"\n      categoryKey: string\n    }>,\n  }),\n})\n\nconst rows = computed(() => data.value.requests)\nconst productLines = computed(() => productLinesData.value.productLines)\n\n// Super-admins narrow against the full taxonomy; non-super reviewers narrow\n// only against the macro keys they actually cover. Omitting `allowedSubCats`\n// signals \"full grid\" to the picker.\n//   - An `all` cell carries the wildcard — any subCat is allowed.\n//   - A `macro` cell contributes its key (an l1 leaf) directly.\n//   - A `micro` cell contributes its parent l1 via `l1KeyFor` so the\n//     picker offers the broader leaf even when the reviewer only owns\n//     a single l2 underneath it.\nconst allowedSubCats = computed<readonly string[] | undefined>(() => {\n  if (adminMe.value.isSuperAdmin) return undefined\n  if (adminMe.value.cells.some((c) => c.categoryLevel === \"all\")) {\n    return undefined\n  }\n  const keys = new Set<string>()\n  for (const c of adminMe.value.cells) {\n    if (c.categoryLevel === \"macro\") keys.add(c.categoryKey)\n    else if (c.categoryLevel === \"micro\") {\n      const parent = l1KeyFor(c.categoryKey)\n      if (parent) keys.add(parent)\n    }\n  }\n  return Array.from(keys)\n})"
}

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\n// Matrix admin surface. The 2026-06-09b redesign widens the gate to\n// `require-reviewer` so any matrix admin (not just super-admins) can\n// reach the page; per-cell authorisation still happens server-side.\n//\n// The unified-table UI lands in the next commit — this revision wires\n// the new 5-coord API and renders the raw cell list so the page is\n// reachable and the data flow is exercised end-to-end before the grid\n// arrives. See `app/components/approvals/ReviewerMatrix.vue` for the\n// upcoming redesign.\ndefinePageMeta({\n  middleware: [\"require-auth\", \"require-reviewer\"],\n})\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\n\nconst { data, pending } = await useFetch(\"/api/internal/admin/reviewers\", {\n  default: () => ({\n    ok: true,\n    admins: [],\n    productLines: [],\n    viewer: { isSuperAdmin: false, coveringCells: [] },\n  }),\n})\n\nconst admins = computed(() => data.value.admins)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"px-6 py-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto\">\n    <header class=\"mb-6\">\n      <h1 class=\"font-serif text-3xl tracking-tight text-(--color-ink)\">\n        {{ t(\"admin.reviewers.title\") }}\n      </h1>\n      <p class=\"mt-1 text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n        {{ t(\"admin.reviewers.subtitle\") }}\n      </p>\n    </header>\n\n    <p v-if=\"pending\" class=\"text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n      {{ t(\"admin.reviewers.loading\") }}\n    </p>\n    <ul v-else class=\"space-y-1 text-sm\">\n      <li\n        v-for=\"row in admins\"\n        :key=\"row.id\"\n        class=\"rounded border border-(--color-border) px-3 py-2 text-(--color-ink)\"\n      >\n        <span class=\"font-mono text-xs text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n          {{ row.extensionCategory }} · {{ row.tier }}\n          {{ row.productLineId ? `· ${row.productLineId}` : \"\" }} ·\n          {{ row.categoryLevel }}:{{ row.categoryKey }}\n        </span>\n        <span class=\"ml-2\">\n          {{ row.userName || row.userEmail }}\n        </span>\n      </li>\n    </ul>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

\"reqAnalysis\" })\n  })\n\n  it(\"rejects an unknown extension category\", () => {\n    expect(() =>\n      AssignAdminSchema.parse({\n        extensionCategory: \"cli\",\n        tier: \"company\",\n        categoryLevel: \"macro\",\n        categoryKey: \"cloud\",\n        userId: \"user_42\",\n      }),\n    ).toThrow()\n  })\n\n  it(\"rejects a macro key that isn't an l1 leaf\", () => {\n    expect(() =>\n      AssignAdminSchema.parse({\n        extensionCategory: \"skills\",\n        tier: \"company\",\n        categoryLevel: \"macro\",\n        categoryKey: \"reqAnalysis\", // l2, not l1\n        userId: \"user_42\",\n      }),\n    ).toThrow(/categoryKey/)\n  })\n\n  it(\"rejects a micro key that isn't an l2 leaf\", () => {\n    expect(() =>\n      AssignAdminSchema.parse({\n        extensionCategory: \"skills\",\n        tier: \"company\",\n        categoryLevel: \"micro\",\n        categoryKey: \"systemDesign\", // l1, not l2\n        userId: \"user_42\",\n      }),\n    ).toThrow(/categoryKey/)\n  })\n\n  it(\"rejects an 'all' level paired with a non-wildcard key\", () => {\n    expect(() =>\n      AssignAdminSchema.parse({\n        extensionCategory: \"skills\",\n        tier: \"company\",\n        categoryLevel: \"all\",\n        categoryKey: \"softDev\",\n        userId: \"user_42\",\n      }),\n    ).toThrow(/categoryKey/)\n  })\n\n  it(\"rejects a productLine assignment without productLineId\", () => {\n    expect(() =>\n      AssignAdminSchema.parse({\n        extensionCategory: \"skills\",\n        tier: \"productLine\",\n        categoryLevel: \"macro\",\n        categoryKey: \"softDev\",\n        userId: \"user_42\",\n      }),\n    ).toThrow(/productLineId/)\n  })\n\n  it(\"rejects a company assignment that includes productLineId\", () => {\n    expect(() =>\n      AssignAdminSchema.parse({\n        extensionCategory: \"skills\",\n        tier: \"company\",\n        productLineId: \"wireless\",\n        categoryLevel: \"macro\",\n        categoryKey: \"softDev\",\n        userId: \"user_42\",\n      }),\n    ).toThrow(/productLineId/)\n  })\n\n  it(\"rejects an unknown tier\", () => {\n    expect(() =>\n      AssignAdminSchema.parse({\n        extensionCategory: \"skills\",\n        tier: \"executive\",\n        categoryLevel: \"macro\",\n        categoryKey: \"cloud\",\n        userId: \"user_42\",\n      }),\n    ).toThrow()\n  })\n})\n\ndescribe(\"UnassignAdminSchema\", () => {\n  it(\"requires a non-empty id\", () => {\n    expect(UnassignAdminSchema.parse({ id: \"adm_1\" })).toEqual({ id: \"adm_1\" })\n    expect(() => UnassignAdminSchema.parse({ id: \"\" })).toThrow()\n  })\n})"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/docs/adr/0001-official-tier-approval-workflow.md",
  "old_string": "## Update 2026-06-09 — tier revocation",
  "new_string": "## Update 2026-06-09b — matrix redesign (ext-type × 3-tier × column)\n\nThe 2026-06-08 + revocation updates left the matrix at 45 cells —\n`9 subCats × (Company + 4 ProductLines)` — with a flat one-level\ncategory axis (FUNC_TAXONOMY l1 only) and exact-match routing. Two\nlimitations surfaced in practice:\n\n1. **No per-extension-type split**. A Skills reviewer and an MCP\n   reviewer overlap if they care about the same subCat, but they\n   review fundamentally different artifacts. The matrix had no way\n   to route them separately.\n2. **Category granularity was either too coarse (l1 only) or\n   inexpressive (l2 was stored on the extension but ignored by\n   routing)**. A reviewer who only knows `reqAnalysis` couldn't sign\n   up just for that leaf; they had to take the whole `systemDesign`.\n\nRefinements (do not revoke the four locked decisions; they remain\naccurate):\n\n- **Decision #1 widens to 5 coordinates**: routing key is now\n  `(extensionCategory × tier × productLineId? × categoryLevel × categoryKey)`.\n  `extensionCategory` reuses the existing `extension_category` enum\n  (`skills | mcp | slash | plugins`); `categoryLevel` is a new\n  `admin_category_level` enum (`all | macro | micro`); `categoryKey`\n  is `'*'` at the `all` level, an l1 leaf at `macro`, an l2 leaf at\n  `micro`. The reviewer table is renamed `approval_admins` because a\n  row now grants both review duty AND admin authority over its\n  covered shadow — see the cover relation below.\n- **Decision #4 widens via a 2-D cover relation**. Matrix edits are\n  gated by `requireCellAdmin(event, cell)` which returns true iff\n  the caller holds an admin row whose covered shadow includes the\n  target cell:\n    - **Column-tier dim** — `(company, null) ⊇ (productLine, X)` for\n      any X. A company admin can configure product-line admins. A\n      PL admin can only configure same-PL cells.\n    - **Category dim** — `(all, '*')` covers everything; `(macro, l1)`\n      covers itself + its 3 l2 children; `(micro, l2)` covers only\n      itself.\n  Cross-extensionCategory authority does NOT exist — each ext-type\n  matrix is independent. Super-admins still bypass the gate.\n- **Routing is fan-out on the category dim, EXACT on column-tier**.\n  A pending request at `(E, T, C, l1, l2)` routes to any admin whose\n  `(extensionCategory, tier, productLineId)` matches exactly AND\n  whose `(categoryLevel, categoryKey)` covers the request's\n  `(macro=l1, micro=l2)` via the ancestor walk. Crucially, a Company\n  admin does NOT review Product-Line requests — the company →\n  product-line widening applies to admin authority only. Routing is\n  implemented as a single JOIN in\n  `approvalsRepo.listPendingForUser`; super-admins read\n  `listAllPending` instead. Request rows snapshot `extensionCategory`\n  and `l2` at submission time so the JOIN doesn't need a hop through\n  `extensions`.\n- **`approval_requests` columns added**: `extension_category` (NOT\n  NULL, mirrors `extensions.category` at submission time) and `l2`\n  (nullable — extensions without an l2 classification still submit;\n  routing fan-out simply omits the `micro` candidate for those).\n  Updated `idx_approval_status_cell` carries both.\n\nImplementation notes:\n\n- **Clean replacement migration** (`0012_matrix_redesign.sql`): drops\n  `approval_reviewers` and `approval_requests`, recreates both under\n  the new shape. Pre-launch product per CLAUDE.md — current cells\n  are seed data, so no backfill story is owed.\n- **Partial unique indexes on `approval_admins`**: two indexes, one\n  per tier (`approval_admins_pl_uq` and `approval_admins_co_uq`),\n  each carrying exactly the columns that participate. Same pattern\n  as the 2026-06-08 indexes, just widened to the 5-coord key.\n- **`/api/v1` contract is unchanged.** The CLI still sees `badge:\n  \"official\"` derived from `officialTier != null`; extensionCategory\n  is already exposed on extension rows; l2 is not surfaced. A future\n  v2 break could expose the new fields; until then the CLI sees\n  nothing new.\n- **Auth gate single-query**: `findCoveringAdmin` builds the cover\n  relation in code (≤2 column shapes × ≤3 category shapes = ≤6 cell\n  candidates), then ORs them into one indexed probe on\n  `idx_admins_user`. Same approach as `isAdminCoveringRequest` for\n  the decide-path gate, so coverage logic lives in one place.\n- **JOIN-driven queue**: `listPendingForUser` is a single SQL\n  statement that expresses the cover relation directly. `DISTINCT`\n  on `r.id` de-dupes when the same user holds two admin rows that\n  both cover a request — the same dedup pattern as today's\n  `listReviewerQueue` Set, just promoted into SQL.\n\nWhat we gave up:\n\n- The l2 keyspace doubles the row count of any admin who wants\n  micro-level granularity. We accept this — the matrix UI groups\n  micros under their macros (`shared/taxonomy.ts:l2KeysFor`) so a\n  full Skills × Wireless reviewer at the l2 level is 27 explicit\n  rows, not the implicit 1.\n- Routing's exact-match on column-tier means a Company admin who\n  also wants to be in the PL request queue must hold both an admin\n  row at Company AND one at each PL they care about. We accept this\n  because the alternative — implicit Company → PL routing — would\n  silently aggregate company admins into every PL queue, which read\n  worse in early prototyping than the explicit assignment.\n\nCross-references for this update:\n\n- Migration: `drizzle/0012_matrix_redesign.sql`\n- Schema: `shared/db/schema/approval.ts` (approvalAdmins,\n  approvalRequests with extensionCategory + l2),\n  `shared/taxonomy.ts` (l1/l2 helpers + `categoryAncestors`)\n- Repository rename: `server/repositories/admins.ts` (was `reviewers.ts`)\n- Auth: `server/utils/auth.ts:requireCellAdmin` (5-coord)\n- Orchestrator: `server/utils/approvals.ts` (submit snapshots\n  category + l2; decide path uses `isAdminCoveringRequest`; queue\n  uses `listPendingForUser`/`listAllPending`)\n- Validators: `shared/validators/approvals.ts:AssignAdminSchema`\n- UI: `app/components/approvals/ReviewerMatrix.vue` (unified table\n  with ext-type toggle + 3-tier rows — lands in the follow-up commit)\n\n## Update 2026-06-09 — tier revocation"
}
```

50	- When a binding decision changes, update `docs/plan.md` in the same commit as the code change.
51	- **No agent-attribution noise in commits or PRs.** Do not add a `Co-Authored-By:` trailer (e.g. `Co-Authored-By: Claude …`) to commit messages, and do not append the "🤖 Generated with Claude Code" footer to PR descriptions. The branch already records authorship and the content speaks for itself.
52	- **Dev sign-in.** `bun run db:seed` plants six demo users on `*@agentcenter.dev` (`amy` = super-admin, the rest publishers / reviewers — see `scripts/seed.ts:CREATORS`). The shared password comes from the `SEED_PASSWORD` env var; the seed fails fast if it isn't set so a misconfigured environment can't hand out a predictable default. Run as `SEED_PASSWORD=[REDACTED] bun run db:seed`. Rotate by re-running with a new env value; the deterministic credential row id makes it idempotent.
53	
54	## Project structure
55	
56	See `docs/plan.md` §1 for the full tree.
57	
58	- `app/pages/...` — file-based routes (locale-prefixed via `@nuxtjs/i18n`)
59	- `app/components/{ui,layout,extension,filters,publish,profile}` — Vue components
60	- `app/composables/`, `app/middleware/` — client state + route guards
61	- `server/api/v1/...` — public registry API consumed by the CLI (frozen contract)
62	- `server/api/internal/...` — form-backing endpoints (Nuxt's analogue of Next.js server actions)
63	- `server/api/{auth,upload,inngest}/...` — Better Auth, upload signing, Inngest webhook
64	- `server/utils/` — server-side singletons and queries
65	- `shared/{db/schema,validators,search,tags,taxonomy,data,types}` — code reachable from both `app/` and `server/`
66	- `cli/` — separate Bun-built binary
67	- `drizzle/` — generated migrations
68	- `docs/` — plan, ADRs, manifest spec
69

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/CLAUDE.md",
  "old_string": "12. **Official-tier approval workflow**: extensions default to Unofficial; elevation to Product-Line Official or Company Official goes through a reviewer matrix indexed by `(tier × FUNC_TAXONOMY subCat)`, additionally keyed by `productLine` (data-driven `product_lines` table; current rows: wireless, datacom, terminals, cloud) for the `productLine` tier only. Tiers are independent (no PL→Company ladder), `officialTier` lives in its own enum column, and the `/api/v1` `badge` field is read-derived from it to preserve the frozen CLI contract. Matrix edits: super-admins everywhere; company-tier admins of a given subCat may manage productLine-tier admins for the same subCat. Revocation is a super-admin action from the detail page; revocation metadata (timestamp / admin / required note) lives on `extensions` and clears on the next approval. Full rationale and trade-offs in `docs/adr/0001-official-tier-approval-workflow.md` (including the 2026-06-08 and 2026-06-09 updates).",
  "new_string": "12. **Official-tier approval workflow**: extensions default to Unofficial; elevation to Product-Line Official or Company Official goes through an admin matrix indexed by 5 coords — `(extensionCategory × tier × productLineId? × categoryLevel × categoryKey)`. `extensionCategory` reuses the existing `extension_category` enum so the matrix is tabbed per ext-type; `categoryLevel` is `all | macro | micro` (wildcard / FUNC_TAXONOMY l1 / l2); `productLineId` participates only for the `productLine` tier. Tiers are independent (no PL→Company ladder), `officialTier` lives in its own enum column, and the `/api/v1` `badge` field is read-derived from it to preserve the frozen CLI contract. Matrix edits use a 2-D cover relation: super-admins everywhere; any user whose admin row covers the target cell on both the column-tier axis (`(company, null) ⊇ (productLine, X)`) and the category axis (`all ⊇ macro ⊇ micro`) within the same extensionCategory. Routing is fan-out on the category dim and EXACT on column-tier (company admins do NOT receive product-line requests for review). Revocation is a super-admin action from the detail page; revocation metadata (timestamp / admin / required note) lives on `extensions` and clears on the next approval. Full rationale and trade-offs in `docs/adr/0001-official-tier-approval-workflow.md` (including the 2026-06-08, 2026-06-09, and 2026-06-09b updates)."
}

{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat(p9-approvals): redesign matrix to ext-type × 3-tier × column\n\nReframes the approval admin matrix per the 2026-06-09b ADR-0001\nupdate. The cell key widens from `(tier × subCat × productLineId?)`\nto a five-dimensional shape:\n\n  (extensionCategory × tier × productLineId? × categoryLevel × categoryKey)\n\nextensionCategory reuses the existing `extension_category` enum so\nthe matrix is tabbed per ext-type. categoryLevel is a new enum\n(`all | macro | micro`) — `all` is the wildcard root, `macro` is a\nFUNC_TAXONOMY l1 leaf, `micro` is an l2 leaf. categoryKey is `'*'`\nat level `all`, an l1 key at `macro`, an l2 key at `micro`.\n\nA row in the renamed `approval_admins` table now grants both review\nduty AND admin authority over its covered shadow, so the table name\nfollows. The cover relation has two independent axes:\n\n  - column-tier:  (company, null) ⊇ (productLine, X) ⊇ self\n  - category:     (all, '*') ⊇ (macro, l1) ⊇ (micro, l2-under-l1)\n\n`requireCellAdmin` is the single gate for matrix edits — it returns\ntrue iff the caller holds an admin row whose covered shadow includes\nthe target cell (within the same extensionCategory). Cross-ext-type\nauthority is intentionally absent. Routing for the reviewer queue\nfans out on the category dim only; column-tier matches exactly, so\ncompany admins do NOT pick up product-line requests for review (the\ncompany → PL widening is admin authority only). Implemented as a\nsingle JOIN in `approvalsRepo.listPendingForUser`.\n\nRequest rows snapshot `extension_category` and `l2` at submission\ntime so the routing JOIN doesn't have to hop through `extensions`,\nmatching the existing precedent for `requested_tier` and `sub_cat`.\n\nMigration 0012 is a clean drop+recreate of both `approval_requests`\nand `approval_reviewers` (renamed to `approval_admins`) — pre-launch\nproduct per CLAUDE.md, so the existing matrix is seed data and no\nbackfill story is owed. The admin matrix UI page is stubbed to a\nflat list for this commit; the unified grid (ext-type toggle +\n3-tier rows + per-cell canEdit) lands in the next commit.\n\nADR-0001 gains a 2026-06-09b update section; CLAUDE.md §12 reflects\nthe new 5-coord shape and the cover-relation wording.\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit 2: schema + backend rewrite"
}
```

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Loader2, X } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	
4	import { FUNC_TAXONOMY } from "~~/shared/taxonomy"
5	
6	// Company-tier matrix view. One column for each subCat × one tier — the
7	// previous productLine column moved to a sibling component, so what stays
8	// here is the simpler list of (subCat → admins). Edits in this view are
9	// super-admin-only; `canEdit=false` cells render the chip controls in a
10	// disabled state and the "+ Add" tile collapses to a read-only marker.
11	
12	export interface CompanyReviewerRow {
13	  id: string
14	  subCat: string
15	  userId: string
16	  userEmail: string
17	  userName: string | null
18	  canEdit: boolean
19	}
20	
21	const props = defineProps<{ reviewers: CompanyReviewerRow[] }>()
22	const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "refresh"): void }>()
23	
24	const { t } = useI18n()
25	
26	const subCats = computed(() =>
27	  FUNC_TAXONOMY.flatMap((cat) => cat.l1.map((leaf) => leaf.key)),
28	)
29	
30	const cells = computed<Record<string, CompanyReviewerRow[]>>(() => {
31	  const out: Record<string, CompanyReviewerRow[]> = {}
32	  for (const r of props.reviewers) {
33	    if (!out[r.subCat]) out[r.subCat] = []
34	    out[r.subCat]!.push(r)
35	  }
36	  return out
37	})
38	
39	function reviewersFor(subCat: string): CompanyReviewerRow[] {
40	  return cells.value[subCat] ?? []
41	}
42	
43	// Add-reviewer flow state. The cell's editability follows the first row's
44	// `canEdit` flag (the server returns the same flag for every row in a
45	// cell); for empty cells we fall back to the injected viewer role.
46	const editingSubCat = ref<string | null>(null)
47	const newEmail = ref("")
48	const lookupError = ref<string | null>(null)
49	const busy = ref(false)
50	
51	function openAdd(subCat: string) {
52	  if (!canEditCellOrEmpty(subCat)) return
53	  editingSubCat.value = subCat
54	  newEmail.value = ""
55	  lookupError.value = null
56	}
57	
58	// Empty cells still need editing when the viewer is a super-admin; we
59	// can't infer canEdit from a row that doesn't exist. The page passes the
60	// viewer's role separately so we can fall back to it here.
61	const viewerIsSuperAdmin = inject<Ref<boolean>>("viewerIsSuperAdmin", ref(false))
62	function canEditCellOrEmpty(subCat: string): boolean {
63	  const rows = reviewersFor(subCat)
64	  if (rows.length > 0) return rows[0]!.canEdit
65	  return viewerIsSuperAdmin.value
66	}
67	
68	function closeAdd() {
69	  editingSubCat.value = null
70	  newEmail.value = ""
71	  lookupError.value = null
72	}
73	
74	async function addReviewer() {
75	  if (!editingSubCat.value || busy.value) return
76	  const email = newEmail.value.trim()
77	  if (!email) return
78	  busy.value = true
79	  lookupError.value = null
80	  try {
81	    const { user } = await $fetch("/api/internal/admin/users/by-email", {
82	      query: { email },
83	    })
84	    if (!user) {
85	      lookupError.value = t("admin.reviewers.matrix.noSuchUser")
86	      return
87	    }
88	    await $fetch("/api/internal/admin/reviewers/assign", {
89	      method: "POST",
90	      body: {
91	        tier: "company",
92	        subCat: editingSubCat.value,
93	        userId: user.id,
94	      },
95	    })
96	    closeAdd()
97	    emit("refresh")
98	  } catch (err) {
99	    console.error("[reviewers] add failed", err)
100	    lookupError.value = t("admin.reviewers.matrix.genericError")
101	  } finally {
102	    busy.value = false
103	  }
104	}
105	
106	async function removeReviewer(row: CompanyReviewerRow) {
107	  if (busy.value || !row.canEdit) return
108	  busy.value = true
109	  try {
110	    await $fetch("/api/internal/admin/reviewers/unassign", {
111	      method: "DELETE",
112	      body: { id: row.id },
113	    })
114	    emit("refresh")
115	  } catch (err) {
116	    console.error("[reviewers] remove failed", err)
117	  } finally {
118	    busy.value = false
119	  }
120	}
121	</script>
122	
123	<template>
124	  <div class="overflow-x-auto">
125	    <table class="w-full border-separate border-spacing-0 text-sm">
126	      <thead>
127	        <tr>
128	          <th class="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-(--color-bg) border-b border-(--color-border) py-2 px-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-(--color-ink-muted)">
129	            {{ t("admin.reviewers.matrix.subCatColumn") }}
130	          </th>
131	          <th class="border-b border-(--color-border) py-2 px-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-(--color-ink)">
132	            {{ t("extensions.officialTier.company") }}
133	          </th>
134	        </tr>
135	      </thead>
136	      <tbody>
137	        <tr v-for="subCat in subCats" :key="subCat">
138	          <th
139	            scope="row"
140	            class="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-(--color-bg) border-b border-(--color-border) py-3 px-3 text-left font-medium text-(--color-ink)"
141	          >
142	            {{ t(`taxonomy.l1.${subCat}`) }}
143	          </th>
144	          <td class="border-b border-(--color-border) py-3 px-3 align-top">
145	            <ul class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
146	              <li
147	                v-for="r in reviewersFor(subCat)"
148	                :key="r.id"
149	                class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) px-2 py-0.5 text-xs"
150	                :class="r.canEdit ? '' : 'opacity-70'"
151	              >
152	                <span class="truncate max-w-[12rem]" :title="r.userEmail">
153	                  {{ r.userName || r.userEmail }}
154	                </span>
155	                <button
156	                  v-if="r.canEdit"
157	                  type="button"
158	                  :aria-label="t('admin.reviewers.matrix.removeAria', { user: r.userEmail })"
159	                  class="text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-red-600"
160	                  @click="removeReviewer(r)"
161	                >
162	                  <X class="size-3" aria-hidden="true" />
163	                </button>
164	              </li>
165	              <li v-if="canEditCellOrEmpty(subCat)">
166	                <button
167	                  type="button"
168	                  class="rounded-full border border-dashed border-(--color-border) px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)/60"
169	                  @click="openAdd(subCat)"
170	                >
171	                  + {{ t("admin.reviewers.matrix.add") }}
172	                </button>
173	              </li>
174	            </ul>
175	          </td>
176	        </tr>
177	      </tbody>
178	    </table>
179	
180	    <Dialog :open="!!editingSubCat" @update:open="(v) => !v && closeAdd()">
181	      <DialogContent v-if="editingSubCat" class="max-w-sm">
182	        <DialogTitle class="font-serif text-lg text-(--color-ink)">
183	          {{ t("admin.reviewers.matrix.addTitle") }}
184	        </DialogTitle>
185	        <DialogDescription class="text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)">
186	          {{
187	            t("admin.reviewers.matrix.addSubtitle", {
188	              tier: t("extensions.officialTier.company"),
189	              subCat: t(`taxonomy.l1.${editingSubCat}`),
190	            })
191	          }}
192	        </DialogDescription>
193	        <div class="space-y-3">
194	          <Label for="add-co-email" class="text-xs font-medium">
195	            {{ t("admin.reviewers.matrix.emailLabel") }}
196	          </Label>
197	          <Input
198	            id="add-co-email"
199	            v-model="newEmail"
200	            type="email"
201	            :placeholder="t('admin.reviewers.matrix.emailPlaceholder')"
202	            @keydown.enter="addReviewer"
203	          />
204	          <p v-if="lookupError" class="text-sm text-red-600">{{ lookupError }}</p>
205	        </div>
206	        <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
207	          <button
208	            type="button"
209	            class="text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)"
210	            @click="closeAdd"
211	          >
212	            {{ t("admin.reviewers.matrix.cancel") }}
213	          </button>
214	          <button
215	            type="button"
216	            :disabled="busy || !newEmail.trim()"
217	            class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-(--color-accent) px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-(--color-accent-fg) hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-50"
218	            @click="addReviewer"
219	          >
220	            <Loader2 v-if="busy" class="size-3.5 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
221	            {{ t("admin.reviewers.matrix.confirmAdd") }}
222	          </button>
223	        </div>
224	      </DialogContent>
225	    </Dialog>
226	  </div>
227	</template>
228
