{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/approvals/ReviewerMatrix.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Loader2, X } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\n\nimport {\n  ALL_L1_KEYS,\n  FUNC_TAXONOMY,\n  l2KeysFor,\n} from \"~~/shared/taxonomy\"\n\n// Redesigned matrix shell — see ADR-0001 (2026-06-09b). The vertical axis\n// is a 3-tier hierarchy (All → Macro = FUNC_TAXONOMY l1 → Micro = l2);\n// the horizontal axis fuses Company and the product lines into one\n// table; and the previous Tier tabs become an `extension_category`\n// toggle. Per-cell `canEdit` flows from the API based on the 2-D cover\n// relation; non-editable cells render the chip controls in a disabled\n// state and skip the \"+ Add\" tile.\n\ntype ExtensionCategory = \"skills\" | \"mcp\" | \"slash\" | \"plugins\"\ntype Tier = \"productLine\" | \"company\"\ntype CategoryLevel = \"all\" | \"macro\" | \"micro\"\nconst EXTENSION_CATEGORIES: readonly ExtensionCategory[] = [\n  \"skills\",\n  \"mcp\",\n  \"slash\",\n  \"plugins\",\n] as const\n\nexport interface AdminCellRow {\n  id: string\n  extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n  tier: Tier\n  productLineId: string | null\n  categoryLevel: CategoryLevel\n  categoryKey: string\n  userId: string\n  userEmail: string\n  userName: string | null\n  canEdit: boolean\n}\n\nexport interface ProductLine {\n  id: string\n  labelEn: string\n  labelZh: string\n  sortOrder: number\n}\n\nexport interface CoveringCell {\n  extensionCategory: ExtensionCategory\n  tier: Tier\n  productLineId: string | null\n  categoryLevel: CategoryLevel\n  categoryKey: string\n}\n\nexport interface Viewer {\n  isSuperAdmin: boolean\n  coveringCells: CoveringCell[]\n}\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  admins: AdminCellRow[]\n  productLines: ProductLine[]\n  viewer: Viewer\n}>()\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ (e: \"refresh\"): void }>()\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\n\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst router = useRouter()\n\nfunction hashToCategory(hash: string): ExtensionCategory {\n  const m = hash.match(/#cat=(skills|mcp|slash|plugins)/)\n  return (m?.[1] as ExtensionCategory) ?? \"skills\"\n}\n\nconst activeCategory = ref<ExtensionCategory>(hashToCategory(route.hash))\n\nwatch(activeCategory, (next) => {\n  router.replace({ hash: `#cat=${next}` })\n})\n\nwatch(\n  () => route.hash,\n  (next) => {\n    const cat = hashToCategory(next)\n    if (activeCategory.value !== cat) activeCategory.value = cat\n  },\n)\n\n// Columns: a fixed \"Company\" column first, then product lines in\n// sortOrder. Each renders against (tier, productLineId).\ninterface Column {\n  key: string\n  tier: Tier\n  productLineId: string | null\n  label: string\n}\nconst columns = computed<Column[]>(() => {\n  const pls = [...props.productLines].sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder)\n  return [\n    {\n      key: \"company\",\n      tier: \"company\",\n      productLineId: null,\n      label: t(\"extensions.officialTier.company\"),\n    },\n    ...pls.map((pl) => ({\n      key: `pl:${pl.id}`,\n      tier: \"productLine\" as const,\n      productLineId: pl.id,\n      label: pl.labelEn === pl.labelZh ? pl.labelEn : pl.labelEn,\n    })),\n  ]\n})\n\n// Macro rows (one per FUNC_TAXONOMY l1 leaf), grouped visually by their\n// funcCat parent for a colour hint along the leading edge.\ninterface MacroRow {\n  funcCat: string\n  macroKey: string\n  microKeys: readonly string[]\n}\nconst macros = computed<MacroRow[]>(() =>\n  FUNC_TAXONOMY.flatMap((node) =>\n    node.l1.map((leaf) => ({\n      funcCat: node.key,\n      macroKey: leaf.key,\n      microKeys: leaf.l2,\n    })),\n  ),\n)\n\n// `expanded[macroKey] = true` reveals the three micro rows nested under\n// it. The toggle uses a chevron — collapsed is the default to keep the\n// page short on first load.\nconst expanded = reactive<Record<string, boolean>>({})\n\n// Index admins by composite cell key — one Map keyed by\n// `cat|tier|pl|level|cKey`. Faster than re-scanning the full array per\n// cell and keeps the template terse.\nfunction cellKey(\n  cat: ExtensionCategory,\n  tier: Tier,\n  productLineId: string | null,\n  level: CategoryLevel,\n  key: string,\n): string {\n  return `${cat}|${tier}|${productLineId ?? \"\"}|${level}|${key}`\n}\nconst adminsByCell = computed<Map<string, AdminCellRow[]>>(() => {\n  const out = new Map<string, AdminCellRow[]>()\n  for (const r of props.admins) {\n    const k = cellKey(\n      r.extensionCategory,\n      r.tier,\n      r.productLineId,\n      r.categoryLevel,\n      r.categoryKey,\n    )\n    if (!out.has(k)) out.set(k, [])\n    out.get(k)!.push(r)\n  }\n  return out\n})\n\nfunction adminsFor(\n  tier: Tier,\n  productLineId: string | null,\n  level: CategoryLevel,\n  key: string,\n): AdminCellRow[] {\n  return (\n    adminsByCell.value.get(\n      cellKey(activeCategory.value, tier, productLineId, level, key),\n    ) ?? []\n  )\n}\n\n// A cell that has no admin rows still needs to know if the viewer is\n// allowed to add one — `coveringCells` carries that signal. Mirrors\n// `findCoveringAdmin` on the server: same extensionCategory + cover on\n// both axes. Centralising the rule here keeps the chip dialog and the\n// \"+ Add\" tile in lockstep.\nfunction viewerCovers(\n  tier: Tier,\n  productLineId: string | null,\n  level: CategoryLevel,\n  key: string,\n): boolean {\n  if (props.viewer.isSuperAdmin) return true\n  for (const c of props.viewer.coveringCells) {\n    if (c.extensionCategory !== activeCategory.value) continue\n    // Column-tier cover: (company, null) ⊇ (productLine, X) ⊇ self.\n    const colOk =\n      (c.tier === \"company\" && c.productLineId === null) ||\n      (c.tier === tier && c.productLineId === productLineId)\n    if (!colOk) continue\n    // Category cover: all/macro/micro ancestor walk.\n    if (c.categoryLevel === \"all\" && c.categoryKey === \"*\") return true\n    if (\n      c.categoryLevel === \"macro\" &&\n      level !== \"all\" &&\n      (key === c.categoryKey || (level === \"micro\" && parentOf(key) === c.categoryKey))\n    ) {\n      return true\n    }\n    if (\n      c.categoryLevel === \"micro\" &&\n      level === \"micro\" &&\n      c.categoryKey === key\n    ) {\n      return true\n    }\n  }\n  return false\n}\n\n// l2 → l1 map for the cover walk above. The wider helper lives in\n// `shared/taxonomy.ts` (`l1KeyFor`); we materialise the lookup once here\n// to avoid the function-call overhead inside the template.\nconst PARENT_OF: ReadonlyMap<string, string> = (() => {\n  const map = new Map<string, string>()\n  for (const macro of FUNC_TAXONOMY) {\n    for (const leaf of macro.l1) {\n      for (const l2 of leaf.l2) map.set(l2, leaf.key)\n    }\n  }\n  return map\n})()\nfunction parentOf(microKey: string): string | undefined {\n  return PARENT_OF.get(microKey)\n}\n\n// Picker dialog state — one dialog shared across every \"+ Add\" tile.\ninterface TargetCell {\n  tier: Tier\n  productLineId: string | null\n  categoryLevel: CategoryLevel\n  categoryKey: string\n}\nconst editingCell = ref<TargetCell | null>(null)\nconst newEmail = ref(\"\")\nconst lookupError = ref<string | null>(null)\nconst busy = ref(false)\n\nfunction openAdd(cell: TargetCell) {\n  if (!viewerCovers(cell.tier, cell.productLineId, cell.categoryLevel, cell.categoryKey)) {\n    return\n  }\n  editingCell.value = cell\n  newEmail.value = \"\"\n  lookupError.value = null\n}\n\nfunction closeAdd() {\n  editingCell.value = null\n  newEmail.value = \"\"\n  lookupError.value = null\n}\n\nasync function addAdmin() {\n  const cell = editingCell.value\n  if (!cell || busy.value) return\n  const email = newEmail.value.trim()\n  if (!email) return\n  busy.value = true\n  lookupError.value = null\n  try {\n    const { user } = await $fetch(\"/api/internal/admin/users/by-email\", {\n      query: { email },\n    })\n    if (!user) {\n      lookupError.value = t(\"admin.reviewers.matrix.noSuchUser\")\n      return\n    }\n    await $fetch(\"/api/internal/admin/reviewers/assign\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      body: {\n        extensionCategory: activeCategory.value,\n        tier: cell.tier,\n        productLineId: cell.productLineId ?? undefined,\n        categoryLevel: cell.categoryLevel,\n        categoryKey: cell.categoryKey,\n        userId: user.id,\n      },\n    })\n    closeAdd()\n    emit(\"refresh\")\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[admins] add failed\", err)\n    lookupError.value = t(\"admin.reviewers.matrix.genericError\")\n  } finally {\n    busy.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function removeAdmin(row: AdminCellRow) {\n  if (busy.value || !row.canEdit) return\n  busy.value = true\n  try {\n    await $fetch(\"/api/internal/admin/reviewers/unassign\", {\n      method: \"DELETE\",\n      body: { id: row.id },\n    })\n    emit(\"refresh\")\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[admins] remove failed\", err)\n  } finally {\n    busy.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nfunction labelForCellHeader(level: CategoryLevel, key: string): string {\n  if (level === \"all\") return t(\"admin.reviewers.matrix.level.all\")\n  if (level === \"macro\") return t(`taxonomy.l1.${key}`)\n  return t(`taxonomy.l2.${key}`)\n}\n\nfunction dialogLevelLabel(cell: TargetCell): string {\n  return labelForCellHeader(cell.categoryLevel, cell.categoryKey)\n}\n\nfunction dialogColumnLabel(cell: TargetCell): string {\n  if (cell.tier === \"company\") return t(\"extensions.officialTier.company\")\n  const pl = props.productLines.find((p) => p.id === cell.productLineId)\n  return pl?.labelEn ?? cell.productLineId ?? \"\"\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"space-y-4\">\n    <!-- Extension-category toggle (4 buttons). Replaces the pre-redesign\n         Company/Product-Line tab pair. -->\n    <div\n      role=\"tablist\"\n      :aria-label=\"t('admin.reviewers.matrix.categoryAxisLabel')\"\n      class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-1\"\n    >\n      <button\n        v-for=\"cat in EXTENSION_CATEGORIES\"\n        :key=\"cat\"\n        type=\"button\"\n        role=\"tab\"\n        :aria-selected=\"activeCategory === cat\"\n        class=\"rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium\"\n        :class=\"\n          activeCategory === cat\n            ? 'bg-(--color-bg) text-(--color-ink) shadow-sm'\n            : 'text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'\n        \"\n        @click=\"activeCategory = cat\"\n      >\n        {{ t(`admin.reviewers.matrix.cat.${cat}`) }}\n      </button>\n    </div>\n\n    <p\n      v-if=\"!viewer.isSuperAdmin\"\n      class=\"text-xs text-(--color-ink-muted)\"\n    >\n      {{ t(\"admin.reviewers.matrix.delegationNote\") }}\n    </p>\n\n    <div class=\"overflow-x-auto\">\n      <table class=\"w-full border-separate border-spacing-0 text-sm\">\n        <thead>\n col.label }}\n            </th>\n          </tr>\n        </thead>\n        <tbody>\n          <!-- All row (wildcard, single sticky row at the top). -->\n          <tr>\n            <th\n              scope=\"row\"\n              class=\"sticky left-0 z-10 bg-(--color-bg) border-b border-(--color-border) py-3 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-(--color-ink)\"\n            >\n              {{ items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-(--color-accent) px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-(--color-accent-fg) hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-50\"\n            @click=\"addAdmin\"\n          >\n </DialogContent>\n    </Dialog>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/admin/reviewers/index.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\n// Matrix admin surface. The 2026-06-09b redesign widens the gate to\n// `require-reviewer` so any matrix admin (not just super-admins) can\n// reach the page; per-cell authorisation still happens server-side.\n// `ReviewerMatrix.vue` is the single unified table — see\n// `app/components/approvals/ReviewerMatrix.vue`.\ndefinePageMeta({\n  middleware: [\"require-auth\", \"require-reviewer\"],\n})\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\n\nconst { data, refresh, pending } = await useFetch(\n  \"/api/internal/admin/reviewers\",\n  {\n    default: () => ({\n      ok: true,\n      admins: [],\n      productLines: [],\n      viewer: { isSuperAdmin: false, coveringCells: [] },\n    }),\n  },\n)\n\nconst admins = computed(() => data.value.admins)\nconst productLines = computed(() => data.value.productLines)\nconst viewer = computed(() => data.value.viewer)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"px-6 py-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto\">\n    <header class=\"mb-6\">\n      <h1 class=\"font-serif text-3xl tracking-tight text-(--color-ink)\">\n        {{ t(\"admin.reviewers.title\") }}\n      </h1>\n      <p class=\"mt-1 text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n        {{ t(\"admin.reviewers.subtitle\") }}\n      </p>\n    </header>\n\n    <p v-if=\"pending\" class=\"text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n      {{ t(\"admin.reviewers.loading\") }}\n    </p>\n    <ReviewerMatrix\n      v-else\n      :admins=\"admins\"\n      :product-lines=\"productLines\"\n      :viewer=\"viewer\"\n      @refresh=\"refresh\"\n    />\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

}).$fetch\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  fetchMock.mockReset()\n  ;(globalThis as unknown as { $fetch: typeof fetchMock }).$fetch = fetchMock\n})\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  ;(globalThis as unknown as { $fetch: unknown }).$fetch = realFetch\n})\n\nconst passthrough = (name: string) =>\n  defineComponent({ name, render() { return h(\"div\", this.$slots.default?.()) } })\n\nconst stubs = {\n  Dialog: passthrough(\"Dialog\"),\n  DialogContent: passthrough(\"DialogContent\"),\n  DialogTitle: passthrough(\"DialogTitle\"),\n  DialogDescription: passthrough(\"DialogDescription\"),\n  Label: defineComponent({\n    name: \"LabelStub\",\n    props: { for: { type: String, required: false } },\n    render() { return h(\"label\", { for: this.for }, this.$slots.default?.()) },\n  }),\n  Input: [\"update:modelValue\"],\n    render() {\n      return h(\"input\", {\n        type: this.type,\n        value: this.modelValue,\n        onInput: (e: Event) =>\n          this.$emit(\"update:modelValue\", (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value),\n      })\n    },\n  }),\n}\n\nconst PRODUCT_LINES = [\n  { id: \"wireless\", labelEn: \"Wireless\", labelZh: \"无线\", sortOrder: 10 },\n  { id: \"datacom\", labelEn: \"Datacom\", labelZh: \"数通\", sortOrder: 20 },\n]\n\nconst SUPER_VIEWER = {\n  isSuperAdmin: true,\n  coveringCells: [],\n}\n\nconst ADMINS = [\n  // Skills × company × all wildcard — Amy.\n  {\n    id: \"adm-skills-co-all\",\n    extensionCategory: \"skills\" as const,\n    tier: \"company\" as const,\n    productLineId: null,\n    categoryLevel: \"all\" as const,\n    categoryKey: \"*\",\n    userId: \"u-amy\",\n    userEmail: \"amy.example.test\",\n    userName: null,\n    canEdit: true,\n  },\n  // Skills × productLine wireless × macro=softDev — Ben.\n  {\n    id: \"adm-skills-pl-macro\",\n    extensionCategory: \"skills\" as const,\n    tier: \"productLine\" as const,\n    productLineId: \"wireless\",\n    categoryLevel: \"macro\" as const,\n    categoryKey: \"softDev\",\n    userId: \"u-ben\",\n    userEmail: \"ben.example.test\",\n    userName: \"Ben Park\",\n    canEdit: true,\n  },\n  // MCP × company × micro=reqAnalysis — Cory. Lives on a different\n  // extensionCategory tab, so the default (skills) view should NOT\n  // surface this row.\n  {\n    id: \"adm-mcp-co-micro\",\n    extensionCategory: \"mcp\" as const,\n    tier: \"company\" as const,\n    productLineId: null,\n    categoryLevel: \"micro\" as const,\n    categoryKey: \"reqAnalysis\",\n    userId: \"u-cory\",\n    userEmail: \"cory.example.test\",\n    userName: \"Cory\",\n    canEdit: true,\n  },\n]\n\ndescribe(\"ReviewerMatrix (unified)\", () => {\n  it(\"renders one column per (Company + product line) for the active ext-type\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ReviewerMatrix, {\n      props: {\n        admins: [],\n        productLines: PRODUCT_LINES,\n        viewer: SUPER_VIEWER,\n      },\n      global: { stubs },\n    })\n    const headers = wrapper.findAll(\"thead th\")\n    // Row-label column + Company + 2 product lines.\n    expect(headers).toHaveLength(4)\n    expect(headers[1]!.text()).toContain(\"Company Official\")\n    expect(headers[2]!.text()).toContain(\"Wireless\")\n    expect(headers[3]!.text()).toContain(\"Datacom\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"renders the All row plus 9 macro rows (micros collapsed by default)\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ReviewerMatrix, {\n      props: {\n        admins: [],\n        productLines: PRODUCT_LINES,\n        viewer: SUPER_VIEWER,\n      },\n      global: { stubs },\n    })\n    // tbody rows = All (1) + macros (9) + micros that are rendered for\n    // expanded macros (0 by default). Vue's v-show keeps collapsed rows\n    // in the DOM, so we count visible ones via [aria-expanded].\n    const macroToggles = wrapper.findAll(\"th[scope='row'] button[aria-expanded]\")\n    expect(macroToggles).toHaveLength(9)\n    expect(\n      macroToggles.every((b) => b.attributes(\"aria-expanded\") === \"false\"),\n    ).toBe(true)\n  })\n\n  it(\"expanding a macro row reveals its three micro rows\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ReviewerMatrix, {\n      props: {\n        admins: [],\n        productLines: PRODUCT_LINES,\n        viewer: SUPER_VIEWER,\n      },\n      global: { stubs },\n    })\n    const sysDesignToggle = wrapper\n      .findAll(\"th[scope='row'] button[aria-expanded]\")\n      .find((b) => b.text().includes(\"System Design\"))\n    await sysDesignToggle!.trigger(\"click\")\n    await nextTick()\n    expect(sysDesignToggle!.attributes(\"aria-expanded\")).toBe(\"true\")\n    // The three l2 children of systemDesign now have visible rows.\n    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain(\"Requirements Analysis\")\n    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain(\"Functional Design\")\n    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain(\"Architecture Design\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"filters cells by extensionCategory — MCP chips don't show in the Skills tab\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ReviewerMatrix, {\n      props: {\n        admins: ADMINS,\n        productLines: PRODUCT_LINES,\n        viewer: SUPER_VIEWER,\n      },\n      global: { stubs },\n    })\n    // Skills tab is default.\n    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain(\"amy.example.test\")\n    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain(\"Ben Park\")\n    expect(wrapper.text()).not.toContain(\"Cory\")\n\n    // Switch to MCP — only Cory's chip surfaces.\n    const mcpTab = wrapper\n      .findAll(\"button[role='tab']\")\n      .find((b) => b.text().includes(\"MCP\"))\n    await mcpTab!.trigger(\"click\")\n    await nextTick()\n    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain(\"Cory\")\n    expect(wrapper.text()).not.toContain(\"amy.example.test\")\n    expect(wrapper.text()).not.toContain(\"Ben Park\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"renders email as the chip label when name is null\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ReviewerMatrix, {\n      props: {\n        admins: ADMINS,\n        productLines: PRODUCT_LINES,\n        viewer: SUPER_VIEWER,\n      },\n      global: { stubs },\n    })\n    // Amy has userName=null — chip falls back to email.\n    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain(\"amy.example.test\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"posts the 5-coord payload when adding to a macro cell\", async () => {\n    fetchMock\n      .mockResolvedValueOnce({\n        user: { id: \"u-dee\", email: \"dee.example.test\", name: \"Dee\" },\n      })\n      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true })\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ReviewerMatrix, {\n      props: {\n        admins: [],\n        productLines: PRODUCT_LINES,\n        viewer: SUPER_VIEWER,\n      },\n      global: { stubs },\n    })\n    // The All-row × Company cell renders its \"+ Add\" button first in\n    // iteration order, then each product-line column. Click the first\n    // one we find that isn't a tab.\n    const addBtn = wrapper\n      .findAll(\"button\")\n      .find((b) => b.text().includes(\"Add\") && !b.attributes(\"role\"))\n    await addBtn!.trigger(\"click\")\n    await nextTick()\n    await wrapper.find('input[type=\"email\"]').setValue(\"dee.example.test\")\n    await wrapper\n      .findAll(\"button\")\n      .find((b) => b.text() === \"Add admin\")!\n      .trigger(\"click\")\n    await nextTick()\n\n    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(\n      2,\n      \"/api/internal/admin/reviewers/assign\",\n      expect.objectContaining({\n        method: \"POST\",\n        body: {\n          extensionCategory: \"skills\",\n          tier: \"company\",\n          productLineId: undefined,\n          categoryLevel: \"all\",\n          categoryKey: \"*\",\n          userId: \"u-dee\",\n        },\n      }),\n    )\n    expect(wrapper.emitted(\"refresh\")).toHaveLength(1)\n  })\n\n  it(\"hides the '+ Add' tile in cells the viewer's coveringCells don't reach\", async () => {\n    // Viewer is a Skills × productLine=wireless × macro=softDev admin\n    // (not a super-admin). They can edit (skills, wireless × any cat\n    // under softDev) and nothing else. The All row's wireless cell\n    // should have NO add button (level='all' is not covered by a\n    // macro=softDev admin).\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ReviewerMatrix, {\n      props: {\n        admins: [],\n        productLines: PRODUCT_LINES,\n        viewer: {\n          isSuperAdmin: false,\n          coveringCells: [\n            {\n              extensionCategory: \"skills\",\n              tier: \"productLine\",\n              productLineId: \"wireless\",\n              categoryLevel: \"macro\",\n              categoryKey: \"softDev\",\n            },\n          ],\n        },\n      },\n      global: { stubs },\n    })\n    // Expand softDev so we can inspect both the macro and micro rows.\n    const softDevToggle = wrapper\n      .findAll(\"th[scope='row'] button[aria-expanded]\")\n      .find((b) => b.text().includes(\"Software Dev\"))\n    await softDevToggle!.trigger(\"click\")\n    await nextTick()\n\n    // Editable cells: macro=softDev × wireless + each of its 3 micro\n    // rows × wireless = 4 add tiles. The Company column on the same rows\n    // is NOT covered (a PL admin can't reach the Company column).\n    const addCount = wrapper\n      .findAll(\"button\")\n      .filter((b) => b.text().includes(\"Add\") && !b.attributes(\"role\"))\n      .length\n    expect(addCount).toBe(4)\n  })\n\n  it(\"DELETE calls unassign with the row id\", async () => {\n    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue({ ok: true })\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ReviewerMatrix, {\n      props: {\n        admins: ADMINS,\n        productLines: PRODUCT_LINES,\n        viewer: SUPER_VIEWER,\n      },\n      global: { stubs },\n    })\n    // Amy's chip in the All × Company cell has the remove button.\n    const removeBtn = wrapper\n      .findAll(\"button[aria-label]\")\n      .find((b) => b.attributes(\"aria-label\")?.includes(\"Remove\"))\n    await removeBtn!.trigger(\"click\")\n    await nextTick()\n    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n      \"/api/internal/admin/reviewers/unassign\",\n      expect.objectContaining({\n        method: \"DELETE\",\n        body: { id: \"adm-skills-co-all\" },\n      }),\n    )\n  })\n})\n"
}
